────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  TOTAL                              → $0.1442
  cache hit rate                       0.0%
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  [PASS] held audio is NOT played once the caller has barged in  — item_one's two frames reached them; item_two's never do
  [PASS] nothing is recorded as released ([])
  [PASS] and the buffer is emptied rather than left for the next response ([])  — a buffer that survives its response is audio from a cancelled turn arriving inside a later one
[Realtime] 🔇 held second item discarded — test
  [PASS] _drop_held_items clears the buffer and the pending release
  [PASS] the hold is bounded (400 chunks)  — past it an item degrades to the old behaviour rather than growing without bound on a model that will not stop talking
  [PASS] flushing an item with nothing held plays nothing and returns 0  — the capped-out and barged-over cases both arrive here
  [PASS] the first attempt at owed substance is allowed  — refusing at zero deletes the recovery this whole path exists for
  [PASS] the same sentence is refused after 2 attempts that were muted  — unbounded is the livelock: every retry looked like the first
  [PASS] a sentence never tried is still refused once the call has spent 3 recoveries  — per-text counting alone never matches a regenerated sentence, so the loop survives it
